TR - 2/4 (Treosulfan conditioning): 4 x 25 mg/kg BX, 3 x 2000 mg/kg TR.
BM (Bone Marrow): 0 weeks post BMT.
In both graphs, the BM group shows high chimerism (around 90-100%). The TR - 2/4 group shows high chimerism (around 90-100%) up to 5 weeks, then drops sharply to near 0% by 9 weeks, remaining low. The BX - 5/5 group shows chimerism near 0% throughout the measured period.

L2399: Reversed K-M Curve for OS in ITT Population. The graph plots Censoring Probability (Y-axis, 0.0 to 1.0) against Time (month) (X-axis, 0 to 60). L2400: Figure 11 displays two histograms showing the frequency distribution of days from randomization to transplantation for the Busulfan and Treosulfan treatment arms.
The X-axis for both histograms represents the number of days (0 to 70), and the Y-axis represents Frequency (0 to 250).
Busulfan Histogram: The distribution is highly skewed towards lower days. The highest frequency (approximately 220) occurs in the 0-10 day bin. Frequencies drop sharply thereafter, with the 10-20 day bin showing approximately 40, and subsequent bins showing very low frequencies.
Treosulfan Histogram: The distribution is also skewed towards lower days. The highest frequency (approximately 210) occurs in the 0-10 day bin. The 10-20 day bin shows approximately 40, and subsequent bins show very low frequencies.
Both histograms suggest that the majority of patients received transplantation within the first 10 days of randomization.

L2405: Diagram illustrating the two-compartment pharmacokinetic model for treosulfan. The central compartment (V1) receives intravenous (IV) administration. L2406: Figure 17 displays four Goodness-of-Fit (GoF) plots for pooled data, comparing observed data (DV) against predicted values (PRED, IPRED, CWRES, and TIME). The plots are labeled DV vs. PRED | run014, DV vs. IPRED | run014, CWRES vs. TIME | run014, and CWRES vs. PRED | run014. All plots show a strong correlation between observed and predicted values, with the median line and 95% confidence intervals (shaded area) closely following the identity line (y=x). The DV vs. PRED plot shows DV on a logarithmic scale (1 to 1000) against PRED (1 to 1000). The DV vs. IPRED plot shows DV on a logarithmic scale (1 to 1000) against IPRED (1 to 1000). The CWRES vs. TIME plot shows CWRES (residuals) on a linear scale (-2.5 to 5.0) against TIME (0 to 60). The CWRES vs. PRED plot shows CWRES (residuals) on a linear scale (-2.5 to 5.0) against PRED (1 to 1000). The plots indicate a good fit of the model to the data.

The plots compare observed data (Observations) against simulated data (Simulations) for Drug Concentration (DV) versus Time (TALD). The DV axis is logarithmic (1 to 1000). The TALD axis ranges from 1 to 12 hours.
The plots are arranged in two rows of three columns:
Top Row (BSA Groups): BSA \le 0.5 \text{ m}^2 (BSAGR 0), 0.5 < \text{BSA} \le 1 \text{ m}^2 (BSAGR 1), and BSA > 1 \text{ m}^2 (BSAGR 2).

L2408: Figure 19 displays four panels of Goodness-of-Fit (GOF) plots stratified by population (Adults and Pediatrics). The plots are arranged in two rows and two columns.

L2411: Figure 22 is a box plot comparing the Area Under the Curve (AUC24h) in the pediatric population across different Body Surface Area (BSA) groups and dosing regimens. The Y-axis represents AUC24h in mcg*h/mL, ranging from 1000 to 1300. The X-axis represents BSA Groups: BSA\le0.7m2, 0.7<BSA\le1.1m2, and BSA>1.1m2. Three dosing regimens are compared: 9.0g/m2 (green), 9.5g/m2 (blue), and 10.0g/m2 (red).
Horizontal reference lines are shown: Red dashed lines indicate the 25th and 75th percentiles of adult AUC24h following 10g/m2. A black dashed line indicates the median of adult AUC24h following 10g/m2 (target exposure).
The plot shows that AUC24h generally increases with increasing BSA group and increasing dose. The 10.0g/m2 regimen consistently yields the highest AUC24h values, often exceeding the upper red dashed line (75th percentile of adult AUC24h following 10g/m2), particularly in the BSA\le0.7m2 group. The 9.0g/m2 regimen generally falls below the upper red dashed line across all BSA groups.

L2415: Figure B displays the Kaplan-Meier estimated Overall Survival (OS) curve for Acute Myeloid Leukemia (AML) patients receiving an unrelated donor transplant, comparing Busulfan (red line) and Treosulfan (blue line). The X-axis represents Days from Randomization (0 to 1638), and the Y-axis represents Survival (0.0 to 1.0). Both curves start at 1.0 survival. The Busulfan curve shows a slightly higher survival rate than the Treosulfan curve throughout the follow-up period, with the difference becoming more pronounced after 1274 days, where Busulfan survival stabilizes around 0.65 and Treosulfan survival stabilizes around 0.45.
L2416: Figure C displays the Kaplan-Meier estimated Overall Survival (OS) curve for Myelodysplastic Syndrome (MDS) patients receiving a related donor transplant, comparing Busulfan (red line) and Treosulfan (blue line). The X-axis represents Days from Randomization (0 to 1638), and the Y-axis represents Survival (0.0 to 1.0). Both curves start at 1.0 survival. The Busulfan curve shows a slightly higher survival rate than the Treosulfan curve throughout the follow-up period, with the difference becoming more pronounced after 1274 days, where Busulfan survival stabilizes around 0.65 and Treosulfan survival stabilizes around 0.45.
